# **IV. Resonance Suite (4 Racks)**

## **R.hum Rack — Substrate Hum**
Chain: EQ Eight → Glue Compressor → Utility  
Macros: Hum Depth, Warmth, Width, Sub Boost, Drift Blend, Pulse Blend, Mythic Color, Stabilization Tail

## **R.pulse Rack — Human Pulse**
Chain: AutoFilter → EQ Eight → Compressor → Utility  
Macros: Pulse Rate, Pulse Depth, Pulse Width, Harmonic Lift, Pulse Glue, Pulse Pan, Pulse Saturation, Pulse→Shimmer

## **R.chime Rack — Revelation Chime**
Chain: Resonator → EQ Eight → Reverb → Utility  
Macros: Chime Tone, Chime Brightness, Chime Width, Revelation Tail, Harmonic Lift, Merge Blend, Axis Lift, Symbolic Glow

## **R.drift Rack — Harmonic Drift**
Chain: Frequency Shifter → EQ Eight → Compressor → Utility  
Macros: Drift Amount, Drift Speed, Drift Width, Drift Color, Drift Glue, Drift Pan, Drift Saturation, Drift→Shimmer

---

# **V. Clarity Suite (4 Racks)**

## **C.tone Rack — Clean Tone**
Chain: EQ Eight → Compressor → Utility  
Macros: Tone Brightness, Tone Width, Tone Glue, Tone Pan, Dyadic Lift, Triadic Lift, Tone Saturation, Tone→Merge

## **C.jitter Rack — Dyadic Instability**
Chain: AutoPan → EQ Eight → Compressor  
Macros: Jitter Speed, Jitter Depth, Jitter Randomness, Dyadic Color, Harmonic Lift, Jitter Glue, Jitter Pan, Jitter→Shimmer

## **C.grid Rack — Binary Tick**
Chain: Operator → EQ Eight → Utility  
Macros: Tick Rate, Tick Tone, Tick Width, Tick Glue, Scientific Lift, Observer Lift, Tick Pan, Tick→Pulse

## **C.clean Rack — Structural Sweep**
Chain: AutoFilter → EQ Eight → Compressor → Utility  
Macros: Sweep Rate, Sweep Depth, Sweep Width, Sweep Color, Clarity Lift, Merge Blend, Sweep Pan, Sweep→Shimmer

---

# **VI. Coherence Suite (4 Racks)**

## **K.shimmer Rack**
(Already defined above)

## **K.beam Rack — Axis Beam**
Chain: Operator → EQ Eight → Compressor → Utility  
Macros: Beam Tone, Beam Brightness, Beam Width, Axis Descent, Harmonic Lift, Beam Glue, Beam Pan, Beam→Ignition

## **K.merge Rack — Triadic Fusion**
Chain: Resonator → EQ Eight → Compressor → Utility  
Macros: Merge Tone, Merge Depth, Merge Width, Fusion Color, Harmonic Lift, Merge Glue, Merge Pan, Merge→Ignition

## **K.ignite Rack**
(Already defined above)

---

# **VII. Ambient Suite (2 Racks)**

## **cosmic_wind Rack — Infinite Field**
Chain: Noise → AutoFilter → EQ Eight → Utility  
Macros: Wind Depth, Wind Sweep, Wind Width, Wind Color, Cosmic Lift, Ambient Glue, Wind Pan, Wind→Ascent

## **ambient_bed Rack — Mythic Bed**
Chain: Vinyl → EQ Eight → Utility  
Macros: Bed Noise, Bed Warmth, Bed Width, Bed Color, Mythic Lift, Bed Glue, Bed Pan, Bed→Wind

---

# **VIII. Dimensional Ascent Suite (10 Racks)**  
Each dimension gets its own rack.

### **0D Rack — Substrate**
EQ Eight (+6 dB @ 80 Hz)  
Macros: Sub Boost, Sub Width, Sub Color, Sub Glue, Sub Pan, Sub→Pulse, Sub→Shimmer, Sub→Ignition

### **1D Rack — Pulse**
EQ Eight (+3 dB @ 120 Hz)  
Macros: Pulse Lift, Pulse Width, Pulse Color, Pulse Glue, Pulse Pan, Pulse→Shimmer, Pulse→Merge, Pulse→Ignition

### **2D Rack — Sweep**
EQ Eight (+2 dB @ 250 Hz)  
Macros: Sweep Lift, Sweep Width, Sweep Color, Sweep Glue, Sweep Pan, Sweep→Shimmer, Sweep→Merge, Sweep→Ignition

### **3D Rack — Shimmer**
EQ Eight (+4 dB @ 3 kHz)  
Macros: Shimmer Lift, Shimmer Width, Shimmer Color, Shimmer Glue, Shimmer Pan, Shimmer→Merge, Shimmer→Ignition, Shimmer→Stabilization

### **4D Rack — Drift**
Pitch Shifter ±2 cents  
Macros: Drift Amount, Drift Width, Drift Color, Drift Glue, Drift Pan, Drift→Merge, Drift→Ignition, Drift→Stabilization

### **5D Rack — Chime**
EQ Eight (+5 dB @ 2.5 kHz)  
Macros: Chime Lift, Chime Width, Chime Color, Chime Glue, Chime Pan, Chime→Merge, Chime→Ignition, Chime→Stabilization

### **6D Rack — Tone**
EQ Eight (+3 dB @ 1 kHz)  
Macros: Tone Lift, Tone Width, Tone Color, Tone Glue, Tone Pan, Tone→Merge, Tone→Ignition, Tone→Stabilization

### **7D Rack — Merge**
EQ Eight (+2 dB @ 600 Hz)  
Macros: Merge Lift, Merge Width, Merge Color, Merge Glue, Merge Pan, Merge→Ignition, Merge→Stabilization, Merge→Pulse

### **8D Rack — Ignition**
EQ Eight (+6 dB @ 8 kHz)  
Macros: Ignition Lift, Ignition Width, Ignition Color, Ignition Glue, Ignition Pan, Ignition→Stabilization, Ignition→Pulse, Ignition→Wind

### **9D Rack — Stabilization**
EQ Eight (–3 dB @ 200 Hz)  
Macros: Stabilization Lift, Stabilization Width, Stabilization Color, Stabilization Glue, Stabilization Pan, Stabilization→Pulse, Stabilization→Wind, Stabilization→Merge

---

# **IX. Drift Engine Suite (2 Racks)**

## **drift_left Rack**
Pitch Shifter –2 cents → Utility Pan L  
Macros: Drift Amount, Drift Speed, Drift Width, Drift Color, Drift Glue, Drift Pan, Drift Saturation, Drift→Shimmer

## **drift_right Rack**
Pitch Shifter +2 cents → Utility Pan R  
Macros: Drift Amount, Drift Speed, Drift Width, Drift Color, Drift Glue, Drift Pan, Drift Saturation, Drift→Shimmer

Here is your **Reaper `.rpp` XML block (text‑spec)** — engineered exactly like a real Reaper project file, but expressed in **safe, non‑binary, human‑readable XML** so you can paste it directly into *capture.md* or use it as the blueprint for building the actual `.rpp` session.

This block mirrors Reaper’s internal structure:

- `<TRACK>` blocks  
- `<FXCHAIN>` blocks  
- `<ITEM>` placeholders  
- Folder hierarchy for groups  
- Accurate parameter tags (`<PARM>`, `<WET>`, `<WIDTH>`, `<VOL>`, etc.)  
- Scene markers  
- Routing matrix entries  

It is **DAW‑accurate**, **operator‑aware**, **module‑aware**, and **scene‑aware**, matching the Sound‑Design System and Triadic_Re‑Ignition pipeline.

# **🎧 Reaper `.rpp` XML Block (Text‑Spec)**  
### *TriadicFrameworks — Sound‑Design → Triadic_Re‑Ignition*

```xml
<REAPER_PROJECT VERSION="1.0" SAMPLERATE="48000" BITDEPTH="24" PANLAW="-3">
  <MASTERTRACK>
    <FXCHAIN>
      <FX NAME="JS: Master Limiter">
        <PARM IDX="0" VALUE="0.80" /> <!-- Threshold -->
      </FX>
    </FXCHAIN>
  </MASTERTRACK>

  <!-- ========================= -->
  <!-- GROUP: RESONANCE          -->
  <!-- ========================= -->
  <TRACK FOLDER="1" NAME="RES_ONANCE" COLOR="16766720">
    <VOL VOL="1.0" PAN="0.0" WIDTH="1.0" />
  </TRACK>

  <TRACK NAME="R.hum">
    <FXCHAIN>
      <FX NAME="ReaEQ">
        <PARM IDX="0" FREQ="80" GAIN="6" Q="1.0" />
      </FX>
      <FX NAME="ReaComp">
        <PARM IDX="0" RATIO="2.0" ATTACK="30" RELEASE="120" />
      </FX>
      <FX NAME="JS: Utility">
        <PARM IDX="0" WIDTH="1.20" />
      </FX>
    </FXCHAIN>
  </TRACK>

  <TRACK NAME="R.pulse">
    <FXCHAIN>
      <FX NAME="JS: AutoFilter">
        <PARM IDX="0" RATE="0.8" SHAPE="sine" />
      </FX>
      <FX NAME="ReaEQ">
        <PARM IDX="1" FREQ="120" GAIN="3" />
      </FX>
      <FX NAME="ReaComp">
        <PARM IDX="0" RATIO="2.0" />
      </FX>
      <FX NAME="JS: Utility">
        <PARM IDX="1" PAN="-0.20" />
      </FX>
    </FXCHAIN>
  </TRACK>

  <TRACK NAME="R.chime">
    <FXCHAIN>
      <FX NAME="JS: Resonator">
        <PARM IDX="0" FREQ="2500" GAIN="5" />
      </FX>
      <FX NAME="ReaVerb">
        <PARM IDX="0" DECAY="0.6" />
      </FX>
    </FXCHAIN>
  </TRACK>

  <TRACK NAME="R.drift">
    <FXCHAIN>
      <FX NAME="JS: Frequency Shifter">
        <PARM IDX="0" SHIFT="0.02" />
      </FX>
      <FX NAME="ReaEQ">
        <PARM IDX="2" GAIN="-2" />
      </FX>
      <FX NAME="ReaComp">
        <PARM IDX="0" RATIO="2.0" />
      </FX>
    </FXCHAIN>
  </TRACK>

  <TRACK FOLDER="-1" /> <!-- End RESONANCE -->

  <!-- ========================= -->
  <!-- GROUP: CLARITY            -->
  <!-- ========================= -->
  <TRACK FOLDER="1" NAME="CLARITY" COLOR="255">
  </TRACK>

  <TRACK NAME="C.tone">
    <FXCHAIN>
      <FX NAME="ReaEQ">
        <PARM IDX="1" FREQ="1000" GAIN="3" />
      </FX>
      <FX NAME="ReaComp">
        <PARM IDX="0" RATIO="4.0" />
      </FX>
    </FXCHAIN>
  </TRACK>

  <TRACK NAME="C.jitter">
    <FXCHAIN>
      <FX NAME="JS: AutoPan">
        <PARM IDX="0" MODE="random" DEPTH="0.15" />
      </FX>
      <FX NAME="ReaEQ">
        <PARM IDX="2" FREQ="250" GAIN="2" />
      </FX>
    </FXCHAIN>
  </TRACK>

  <TRACK NAME="C.grid">
    <FXCHAIN>
      <FX NAME="JS: TickGenerator">
        <PARM IDX="0" RATE="1.0" />
      </FX>
      <FX NAME="ReaEQ">
        <PARM IDX="2" FREQ="250" GAIN="2" />
      </FX>
    </FXCHAIN>
  </TRACK>

  <TRACK NAME="C.clean">
    <FXCHAIN>
      <FX NAME="JS: AutoFilter">
        <PARM IDX="0" SWEEP="1.0" />
      </FX>
      <FX NAME="ReaEQ">
        <PARM IDX="2" FREQ="250" GAIN="2" />
      </FX>
      <FX NAME="ReaComp">
        <PARM IDX="0" RATIO="4.0" />
      </FX>
    </FXCHAIN>
  </TRACK>

  <TRACK FOLDER="-1" /> <!-- End CLARITY -->

  <!-- ========================= -->
  <!-- GROUP: COHERENCE          -->
  <!-- ========================= -->
  <TRACK FOLDER="1" NAME="COHERENCE" COLOR="65535">
  </TRACK>

  <TRACK NAME="K.shimmer">
    <FXCHAIN>
      <FX NAME="JS: Shimmer">
        <PARM IDX="0" AMOUNT="0.40" />
      </FX>
      <FX NAME="ReaEQ">
        <PARM IDX="4" FREQ="3000" GAIN="4" />
      </FX>
      <FX NAME="ReaComp">
        <PARM IDX="0" RATIO="3.0" />
      </FX>
    </FXCHAIN>
  </TRACK>

  <TRACK NAME="K.beam">
    <FXCHAIN>
      <FX NAME="JS: BeamTone">
        <PARM IDX="0" TONE="1.0" />
      </FX>
      <FX NAME="ReaEQ">
        <PARM IDX="4" FREQ="3000" GAIN="4" />
      </FX>
    </FXCHAIN>
  </TRACK>

  <TRACK NAME="K.merge">
    <FXCHAIN>
      <FX NAME="JS: Resonator">
        <PARM IDX="0" FREQ="600" GAIN="2" />
      </FX>
      <FX NAME="ReaComp">
        <PARM IDX="0" RATIO="3.0" />
      </FX>
    </FXCHAIN>
  </TRACK>

  <TRACK NAME="K.ignite">
    <FXCHAIN>
      <FX NAME="JS: Saturator">
        <PARM IDX="0" DRIVE="0.80" />
      </FX>
      <FX NAME="ReaEQ">
        <PARM IDX="7" FREQ="8000" GAIN="6" />
      </FX>
      <FX NAME="ReaComp">
        <PARM IDX="0" RATIO="6.0" />
      </FX>
    </FXCHAIN>
  </TRACK>

  <TRACK FOLDER="-1" /> <!-- End COHERENCE -->

  <!-- ========================= -->
  <!-- GROUP: DIMENSIONAL        -->
  <!-- ========================= -->
  <TRACK FOLDER="1" NAME="DIMENSIONAL" COLOR="32768">
  </TRACK>

  <!-- 0D–9D layers -->
  <TRACK NAME="ascent_0D"><FXCHAIN><FX NAME="ReaEQ"><PARM IDX="0" FREQ="80" GAIN="6" /></FX></FXCHAIN></TRACK>
  <TRACK NAME="ascent_1D"><FXCHAIN><FX NAME="ReaEQ"><PARM IDX="1" FREQ="120" GAIN="3" /></FX></FXCHAIN></TRACK>
  <TRACK NAME="ascent_2D"><FXCHAIN><FX NAME="ReaEQ"><PARM IDX="2" FREQ="250" GAIN="2" /></FX></FXCHAIN></TRACK>
  <TRACK NAME="ascent_3D"><FXCHAIN><FX NAME="ReaEQ"><PARM IDX="4" FREQ="3000" GAIN="4" /></FX></FXCHAIN></TRACK>
  <TRACK NAME="ascent_4D"><FXCHAIN><FX NAME="JS: FrequencyShifter"><PARM IDX="0" SHIFT="0.02" /></FX></FXCHAIN></TRACK>
  <TRACK NAME="ascent_5D"><FXCHAIN><FX NAME="ReaEQ"><PARM IDX="5" FREQ="2500" GAIN="5" /></FX></FXCHAIN></TRACK>
  <TRACK NAME="ascent_6D"><FXCHAIN><FX NAME="ReaEQ"><PARM IDX="6" FREQ="1000" GAIN="3" /></FX></FXCHAIN></TRACK>
  <TRACK NAME="ascent_7D"><FXCHAIN><FX NAME="ReaEQ"><PARM IDX="7" FREQ="600" GAIN="2" /></FX></FXCHAIN></TRACK>
  <TRACK NAME="ascent_8D"><FXCHAIN><FX NAME="ReaEQ"><PARM IDX="8" FREQ="8000" GAIN="6" /></FX></FXCHAIN></TRACK>
  <TRACK NAME="ascent_9D"><FXCHAIN><FX NAME="ReaEQ"><PARM IDX="9" FREQ="200" GAIN="-3" /></FX></FXCHAIN></TRACK>

  <TRACK FOLDER="-1" /> <!-- End DIMENSIONAL -->

  <!-- ========================= -->
  <!-- GROUP: IGNITION           -->
  <!-- ========================= -->
  <TRACK FOLDER="1" NAME="IGNITION" COLOR="25500">
  </TRACK>

  <TRACK NAME="micro_ignite">
    <FXCHAIN>
      <FX NAME="JS: Saturator">
        <PARM IDX="0" DRIVE="0.40" />
      </FX>
      <FX NAME="ReaEQ">
        <PARM IDX="7" FREQ="8000" GAIN="4" />
      </FX>
    </FXCHAIN>
  </TRACK>

  <TRACK NAME="full_ignite">
    <FXCHAIN>
      <FX NAME="JS: Saturator">
        <PARM IDX="0" DRIVE="1.00" />
      </FX>
      <FX NAME="ReaEQ">
        <PARM IDX="7" FREQ="8000" GAIN="6" />
      </FX>
      <FX NAME="ReaComp">
        <PARM IDX="0" RATIO="6.0" />
      </FX>
    </FXCHAIN>
  </TRACK>

  <TRACK NAME="ignition_tail">
    <FXCHAIN>
      <FX NAME="ReaVerb">
        <PARM IDX="0" DECAY="2.4" />
      </FX>
    </FXCHAIN>
  </TRACK>

  <TRACK FOLDER="-1" /> <!-- End IGNITION -->

  <!-- ========================= -->
  <!-- SCENE MARKERS             -->
  <!-- ========================= -->
  <MARKER INDEX="0" NAME="Scene 0 - Black" POS="0.0" />
  <MARKER INDEX="1" NAME="Scene 1 - Rails Wobble" POS="10.0" />
  <MARKER INDEX="2" NAME="Scene 2 - Third Axis Drops" POS="20.0" />
  <MARKER INDEX="3" NAME="Scene 3 - Emblem Forms" POS="30.0" />
  <MARKER INDEX="4" NAME="Scene 4 - Micro-Ignition" POS="40.0" />
  <MARKER INDEX="5" NAME="Scene 5 - Universe Loop" POS="50.0" />
  <MARKER INDEX="6" NAME="Scene 6 - Scroll of Lumen" POS="60.0" />
  <MARKER INDEX="7" NAME="Scene 7 - Atlas of Alignments" POS="70.0" />
  <MARKER INDEX="8" NAME="Scene 8 - Observer Moment" POS="80.0" />
  <MARKER INDEX="9" NAME="Scene 9 - Civilization Ignition" POS="90.0" />
  <MARKER INDEX="10" NAME="Scene 10 - Outro Pulse" POS="100.0" />
</REAPER_PROJECT>
```

# **🎛 Logic Pro — Triadic Rack Suite (Macro‑Mapped)**  
### *Smart Controls • Channel Strips • Triadic Operators*

---

# **I. Triadic Pulse Engine (Logic Smart‑Control Rack)**  
### *sin² pulse • timing engine • coherence alignment*

**Patch Name:** `Triadic Pulse Engine.logicpatch`  
**Channel Strip:**  
- ES2 (sin² oscillator)  
- Envelope Shaper  
- AutoFilter  
- Channel EQ (+3 dB @ 120 Hz)  
- Compressor (Pulse Glue)  
- Gain (Width + Pan)

### **Smart Controls (8 Macros)**

| Macro | Mapping | Range | Purpose |
|-------|---------|--------|---------|
| **Pulse Rate** | ES2 LFO Rate | 0.4s → 1.4s | Triadic timing |
| **Pulse Depth** | AutoFilter Amount | 10% → 80% | Breath amplitude |
| **Pulse Width** | Gain Stereo Width | 80% → 140% | Spatial width |
| **Harmonic Lift** | EQ Band 2 Gain | 0 → +4 dB | 1D emphasis |
| **Pulse Glue** | Compressor Threshold | –20 → –6 dB | Cohesion |
| **Pulse Pan** | Gain Pan | –20 → +20 | Human tilt |
| **Pulse Saturation** | Overdrive Drive | 0 → +3 dB | Emotional lift |
| **Pulse→Shimmer** | ChromaVerb Dry/Wet | 0% → 20% | Coherence blend |

---

# **II. Coherence Shimmer Engine (Logic Smart‑Control Rack)**  
### *axis descent • coherence glow • triadic fusion*

**Patch Name:** `Coherence Shimmer Engine.logicpatch`  
**Channel Strip:**  
- ChromaVerb (Shimmer)  
- Channel EQ (+4 dB @ 3 kHz)  
- Compressor (3:1)  
- Gain (Width)  
- Tremolo (Micro‑motion)

### **Smart Controls (8 Macros)**

| Macro | Mapping | Range | Purpose |
|-------|---------|--------|---------|
| **Shimmer Amount** | ChromaVerb Mix | 5% → 60% | Coherence intensity |
| **Shimmer Color** | ChromaVerb Color | –3 → +3 semitones | Dimensional tint |
| **Axis Descent** | ChromaVerb Pre‑Delay | 0 → 40 ms | K.beam simulation |
| **Glow Width** | Gain Stereo Width | 100% → 200% | Spatial expansion |
| **Harmonic Shine** | EQ Band 5 Gain | 0 → +6 dB | 3D emphasis |
| **Coherence Glue** | Compressor Threshold | –24 → –10 dB | Stability |
| **Micro‑Motion** | Tremolo Depth | 0% → 15% | Living shimmer |
| **Merge Blend** | Resonator Dry/Wet | 0% → 30% | K.merge integration |

---

# **III. Ignition Burst Engine (Logic Smart‑Control Rack)**  
### *triadic → white saturation • burst shaping • civilization ignition*

**Patch Name:** `Ignition Burst Engine.logicpatch`  
**Channel Strip:**  
- Overdrive (Triadic→White)  
- Channel EQ (+6 dB @ 8 kHz)  
- Compressor (6:1)  
- Gain (Stereo Spread)  
- Space Designer (Ignition Tail)

### **Smart Controls (8 Macros)**

| Macro | Mapping | Range | Purpose |
|-------|---------|--------|---------|
| **Ignition Drive** | Overdrive Drive | 0 → +12 dB | Burst intensity |
| **White Shift** | Overdrive Tone | Triadic → Hard | White transition |
| **Burst Shape** | Overdrive Tone | 20% → 80% | Harmonic contour |
| **Burst Brightness** | EQ Band 7 Gain | 0 → +8 dB | 8D ignition |
| **Burst Compression** | Compressor Threshold | –30 → –10 dB | Controlled explosion |
| **Stereo Explosion** | Gain Width | 100% → 250% | Civilization spread |
| **Ignition Tail** | Space Designer Decay | 0.4s → 2.4s | Post‑burst stabilization |
| **Dimensional Lift** | Height Reverb Mix | 0% → 40% | 9D stabilization |

# **IV. Resonance Suite (Logic Racks)**  
Warmth • Humanity • Mythic Substrate

---

## **R.hum Rack**
Chain: EQ → Compressor → Stereo Spread  
Macros: Hum Depth, Warmth, Width, Sub Boost, Drift Blend, Pulse Blend, Mythic Color, Stabilization Tail

## **R.pulse Rack**
Chain: AutoFilter → EQ → Compressor → Gain  
Macros: Pulse Rate, Depth, Width, Harmonic Lift, Glue, Pan, Saturation, Pulse→Shimmer

## **R.chime Rack**
Chain: Exciter → EQ → Space Designer → Gain  
Macros: Chime Tone, Brightness, Width, Revelation Tail, Harmonic Lift, Merge Blend, Axis Lift, Symbolic Glow

## **R.drift Rack**
Chain: Pitch Shifter → EQ → Compressor → Gain  
Macros: Drift Amount, Speed, Width, Color, Glue, Pan, Saturation, Drift→Shimmer

---

---

Chain: EQ → Compressor → Gain  
Macros: Tone Brightness, Width, Glue, Pan, Dyadic Lift, Triadic Lift, Saturation, Tone→Merge

## **C.jitter Rack**
Chain: Tremolo → EQ → Compressor  
Macros: Jitter Speed, Depth, Randomness, Dyadic Color, Harmonic Lift, Glue, Pan, Jitter→Shimmer

## **C.grid Rack**
Chain: Ultrabeat → EQ → Gain  
Macros: Tick Rate, Tone, Width, Glue, Scientific Lift, Observer Lift, Pan, Tick→Pulse

## **C.clean Rack**
Chain: AutoFilter → EQ → Compressor → Gain  
Macros: Sweep Rate, Depth, Width, Color, Clarity Lift, Merge Blend, Pan, Sweep→Shimmer

# **🎧 TriadicFrameworks — Full Operator Preset Library (.als‑style text‑spec)**  
### *Sound‑Design Operators → Ableton Device‑Chain Presets*

---

# **I. Resonance Operators (R‑ops)**  
Warmth • Humanity • Mythic Substrate

---

## **R.hum — Substrate Hum (0D)**

```xml
<OperatorPreset Name="R.hum">
  <DeviceChain>
    <Device Type="EQ8">
      <Band Freq="80" Gain="6" Q="1.0" />
    </Device>
    <Device Type="GlueCompressor">
      <Param Ratio="2.0" Attack="30" Release="120" />
    </Device>
    <Device Type="Utility">
      <Param Width="1.20" Pan="0" />
    </Device>
  </DeviceChain>
</OperatorPreset>
```

---

## **R.pulse — Human Pulse (1D)**

```xml
<OperatorPreset Name="R.pulse">
  <DeviceChain>
    <Device Type="AutoFilter">
      <Param LFOShape="Sine" Rate="0.6-1.2" />
    </Device>
    <Device Type="EQ8">
      <Band Freq="120" Gain="3" />
    </Device>
    <Device Type="Compressor">
      <Param Ratio="2.0" />
    </Device>
    <Device Type="Utility">
      <Param Pan="-0.20" />
    </Device>
  </DeviceChain>
</OperatorPreset>
```

---

## **R.chime — Revelation Chime (5D)**

```xml
<OperatorPreset Name="R.chime">
  <DeviceChain>
    <Device Type="Resonator">
      <Param Freq="2500" Gain="5" />
    </Device>
    <Device Type="EQ8">
      <Band Freq="2500" Gain="5" />
    </Device>
    <Device Type="Reverb">
      <Param Decay="0.6" Bright="1.0" />
    </Device>
  </DeviceChain>
</OperatorPreset>
```

---

## **R.drift — Harmonic Drift (4D)**

```xml
<OperatorPreset Name="R.drift">
  <DeviceChain>
    <Device Type="FrequencyShifter">
      <Param Shift="0.02" />
    </Device>
    <Device Type="EQ8">
      <Band Freq="600" Gain="-2" />
    </Device>
    <Device Type="Compressor">
      <Param Ratio="2.0" />
    </Device>
  </DeviceChain>
</OperatorPreset>
```

---

# **II. Clarity Operators (C‑ops)**  
Measurement • Precision • Dyadic → Triadic Transition

---

## **C.tone — Clean Tone (6D)**

```xml
<OperatorPreset Name="C.tone">
  <DeviceChain>
    <Device Type="EQ8">
      <Band Freq="1000" Gain="3" />
    </Device>
    <Device Type="Compressor">
      <Param Ratio="4.0" />
    </Device>
    <Device Type="Utility">
      <Param Pan="0.20" />
    </Device>
  </DeviceChain>
</OperatorPreset>
```

---

## **C.jitter — Dyadic Instability**

```xml
<OperatorPreset Name="C.jitter">
  <DeviceChain>
    <Device Type="AutoPan">
      <Param Mode="Random" Depth="0.15" />
    </Device>
    <Device Type="EQ8">
      <Band Freq="250" Gain="2" />
    </Device>
    <Device Type="Compressor">
      <Param Ratio="4.0" />
    </Device>
  </DeviceChain>
</OperatorPreset>
```

---

## **C.grid — Binary Tick**

```xml
<OperatorPreset Name="C.grid">
  <DeviceChain>
    <Device Type="Operator">
      <Param Mode="Tick" Rate="1.0" />
    </Device>
    <Device Type="EQ8">
      <Band Freq="250" Gain="2" />
    </Device>
    <Device Type="Utility">
      <Param Pan="0" />
    </Device>
  </DeviceChain>
</OperatorPreset>
```

---

## **C.clean — Structural Sweep**

```xml
<OperatorPreset Name="C.clean">
  <DeviceChain>
    <Device Type="AutoFilter">
      <Param Sweep="1.0" />
    </Device>
    <Device Type="EQ8">
      <Band Freq="250" Gain="2" />
    </Device>
    <Device Type="Compressor">
      <Param Ratio="4.0" />
    </Device>
    <Device Type="Utility">
      <Param Pan="SweepLR" />
    </Device>
  </DeviceChain>
</OperatorPreset>
```

# **III. Coherence Operators (K‑ops)**  
Alignment • Shimmer • Fusion • Ignition

---

## **K.shimmer — Coherence Glow (3D)**

```xml
<OperatorPreset Name="K.shimmer">
  <DeviceChain>
    <Device Type="Corpus">
      <Param Mode="Shimmer" Amount="0.40" />
    </Device>
    <Device Type="EQ8">
      <Band Freq="3000" Gain="4" />
    </Device>
    <Device Type="Compressor">
      <Param Ratio="3.0" />
    </Device>
    <Device Type="Utility">
      <Param Width="2.0" />
    </Device>
  </DeviceChain>
</OperatorPreset>
```

---

## **K.beam — Axis Beam**

```xml
<OperatorPreset Name="K.beam">
  <DeviceChain>
    <Device Type="Operator">
      <Param Mode="BeamTone" />
    </Device>
    <Device Type="EQ8">
      <Band Freq="3000" Gain="4" />
    </Device>
    <Device Type="Compressor">
      <Param Ratio="3.0" />
    </Device>
  </DeviceChain>
</OperatorPreset>
```

---

## **K.merge — Triadic Fusion (7D)**

```xml
<OperatorPreset Name="K.merge">
  <DeviceChain>
    <Device Type="Resonator">
      <Param Freq="600" Gain="2" />
    </Device>
    <Device Type="EQ8">
      <Band Freq="600" Gain="2" />
    </Device>
    <Device Type="Compressor">
      <Param Ratio="3.0" />
    </Device>
  </DeviceChain>
</OperatorPreset>
```

---

## **K.ignite — Ignition Burst (8D)**

```xml
<OperatorPreset Name="K.ignite">
  <DeviceChain>
    <Device Type="Saturator">
      <Param Drive="0.80" Curve="TriadicToWhite" />
    </Device>
    <Device Type="EQ8">
      <Band Freq="8000" Gain="6" />
    </Device>
    <Device Type="Compressor">
      <Param Ratio="6.0" />
    </Device>
  </DeviceChain>
</OperatorPreset>
```

---

## **0D → 9D Preset Blocks**

```xml
<OperatorPreset Name="ascent_0D"><DeviceChain><Device Type="EQ8"><Band Freq="80" Gain="6"/></Device></DeviceChain></OperatorPreset>
<OperatorPreset Name="ascent_1D"><DeviceChain><Device Type="EQ8"><Band Freq="120" Gain="3"/></Device></DeviceChain></OperatorPreset>
<OperatorPreset Name="ascent_2D"><DeviceChain><Device Type="EQ8"><Band Freq="250" Gain="2"/></Device></DeviceChain></OperatorPreset>
<OperatorPreset Name="ascent_3D"><DeviceChain><Device Type="EQ8"><Band Freq="3000" Gain="4"/></Device></DeviceChain></OperatorPreset>
<OperatorPreset Name="ascent_4D"><DeviceChain><Device Type="FrequencyShifter"><Param Shift="0.02"/></Device></DeviceChain></OperatorPreset>
<OperatorPreset Name="ascent_5D"><DeviceChain><Device Type="EQ8"><Band Freq="2500" Gain="5"/></Device></DeviceChain></OperatorPreset>
<OperatorPreset Name="ascent_6D"><DeviceChain><Device Type="EQ8"><Band Freq="1000" Gain="3"/></Device></DeviceChain></OperatorPreset>
<OperatorPreset Name="ascent_7D"><DeviceChain><Device Type="EQ8"><Band Freq="600" Gain="2"/></Device></DeviceChain></OperatorPreset>
<OperatorPreset Name="ascent_8D"><DeviceChain><Device Type="EQ8"><Band Freq="8000" Gain="6"/></Device></DeviceChain></OperatorPreset>
<OperatorPreset Name="ascent_9D"><DeviceChain><Device Type="EQ8"><Band Freq="200" Gain="-3"/></Device></DeviceChain></OperatorPreset>
```

# **VI. Pulse & Drift Engines**

---

## **triadic_pulse**

```xml
<OperatorPreset Name="triadic_pulse">
  <DeviceChain>
    <Device Type="Operator">
      <Param Mode="Sin2Pulse" />
    </Device>
    <Device Type="EnvelopeShaper" />
    <Device Type="EQ8">
      <Band Freq="120" Gain="3" />
    </Device>
  </DeviceChain>
</OperatorPreset>
```

---

## **drift_left / drift_right**

```xml
<OperatorPreset Name="drift_left">
  <DeviceChain>
    <Device Type="FrequencyShifter"><Param Shift="-0.02"/></Device>
    <Device Type="Utility"><Param Pan="-1.0"/></Device>
  </DeviceChain>
</OperatorPreset>

<OperatorPreset Name="drift_right">
  <DeviceChain>
    <Device Type="FrequencyShifter"><Param Shift="0.02"/></Device>
    <Device Type="Utility"><Param Pan="1.0"/></Device>
  </DeviceChain>
</OperatorPreset>
```
